## What This Check Proves

This check verifies that every visible Guided Reading book has title-page normalization and that story page images stay mechanically aligned with story page numbers. | moonwood-tales-c-01 | 6 | 5 | yes | yes | /guided-reading/series/moonwood-tales/book-01/page-005.webp | At Tumblerock Cliffs, he chooses a low wall above a thick mat. |

| moonwood-tales-c-01 | 9 | 8 | yes | yes | /guided-reading/series/moonwood-tales/book-01/page-008.webp | Reeds rustle beside him. Pip keeps to the marked path. |
| moonwood-tales-c-01 | 10 | 9 | yes | yes | /guided-reading/series/moonwood-tales/book-01/page-009.webp | That evening, the stone's borrowed gold light fades. |
| moonwood-tales-c-01 | 11 | 10 | yes | yes | /guided-reading/series/moonwood-tales/book-01/page-010.webp | "Did its brave run out?" Pip asks Burrow. |

| moonwood-tales-c-01 | 13 | 12 | yes | yes | /guided-reading/series/moonwood-tales/book-01/page-012.webp | Pip keeps the plain stone to remember what his own hands did. |

| moonwood-tales-c-02 | 4 | 3 | yes | yes | /guided-reading/series/moonwood-tales/book-02/page-003.webp | By morning, leaves fill the doorway and curl through every window. |
| moonwood-tales-c-02 | 5 | 4 | yes | yes | /guided-reading/series/moonwood-tales/book-02/page-004.webp | Fern tries slowing and stopping spells. The unfinished plant grows faster. |

| moonwood-tales-c-02 | 8 | 7 | yes | yes | /guided-reading/series/moonwood-tales/book-02/page-007.webp | Fern notices the plant repeats the unfinished tune in rustling leaves. |
| moonwood-tales-c-02 | 9 | 8 | yes | yes | /guided-reading/series/moonwood-tales/book-02/page-008.webp | She sings the final verse her mother taught her. |
| moonwood-tales-c-02 | 10 | 9 | yes | yes | /guided-reading/series/moonwood-tales/book-02/page-009.webp | The stem lowers. Window leaves fold toward the garden. |
| moonwood-tales-c-02 | 11 | 10 | yes | yes | /guided-reading/series/moonwood-tales/book-02/page-010.webp | By evening, one large plant stands beside the open doorway. |

| moonwood-tales-c-02 | 13 | 12 | yes | yes | /guided-reading/series/moonwood-tales/book-02/page-012.webp | Fern sings the missing final notes. Settled leaves rustle them back. |

| moonwood-tales-c-04 | 2 | 1 | yes | yes | /guided-reading/series/moonwood-tales/book-04/page-001.webp | Each morning, Glimmer practises one safe breath inside a stone circle. |
| moonwood-tales-c-04 | 3 | 2 | yes | yes | /guided-reading/series/moonwood-tales/book-04/page-002.webp | The first breath brings smoke, but no flame. |
| moonwood-tales-c-04 | 4 | 3 | yes | yes | /guided-reading/series/moonwood-tales/book-04/page-003.webp | The next breath makes one spark that quickly fades. |
| moonwood-tales-c-04 | 5 | 4 | yes | yes | /guided-reading/series/moonwood-tales/book-04/page-004.webp | Spark says, "Push harder." Thick smoke covers both friends. |
| moonwood-tales-c-04 | 6 | 5 | yes | yes | /guided-reading/series/moonwood-tales/book-04/page-005.webp | A slower breath comes out warm and cinnamon-scented. |

| moonwood-tales-c-04 | 9 | 8 | yes | yes | /guided-reading/series/moonwood-tales/book-04/page-008.webp | Glimmer stops. Forcing more breaths has made their throat sore. |
| moonwood-tales-c-04 | 10 | 9 | yes | yes | /guided-reading/series/moonwood-tales/book-04/page-009.webp | A marsh fox grabs Fern's seed pouch and runs. |
| moonwood-tales-c-04 | 11 | 10 | yes | yes | /guided-reading/series/moonwood-tales/book-04/page-010.webp | Glimmer blocks the path, plants both feet, and warns the fox. |
| moonwood-tales-c-04 | 12 | 11 | yes | yes | /guided-reading/series/moonwood-tales/book-04/page-011.webp | One sudden orange flare makes it drop the seeds and flee. |
| moonwood-tales-c-04 | 13 | 12 | yes | yes | /guided-reading/series/moonwood-tales/book-04/page-012.webp | Glimmer cannot repeat the flare. Pip marks it as a supervised next step. |

| moonwood-tales-c-05 | 13 | 12 | yes | yes | /guided-reading/series/moonwood-tales/book-05/page-012.webp | She shelves that spell and opens a book about safe step stools. |

| moonwood-tales-c-06 | 8 | 7 | yes | yes | /guided-reading/series/moonwood-tales/book-06/page-007.webp | Flint sits. A perfect map of yesterday cannot guide today. |
| moonwood-tales-c-06 | 9 | 8 | yes | yes | /guided-reading/series/moonwood-tales/book-06/page-008.webp | He dates a fresh page and marks the path's new bend. |

| moonwood-tales-c-06 | 11 | 10 | yes | yes | /guided-reading/series/moonwood-tales/book-06/page-010.webp | Pip asks whether the map worked. Flint says, "The new one does." |
| moonwood-tales-c-06 | 12 | 11 | yes | yes | /guided-reading/series/moonwood-tales/book-06/page-011.webp | He pins the first map up under a label: YESTERDAY. |
| moonwood-tales-c-06 | 13 | 12 | yes | yes | /guided-reading/series/moonwood-tales/book-06/page-012.webp | Outside, paths shift. Flint adds today's line to his growing atlas. |

| moonwood-tales-c-07 | 4 | 3 | yes | yes | /guided-reading/series/moonwood-tales/book-07/page-003.webp | It claims to be Moonwood's oldest fish, though it is very small. |
| moonwood-tales-c-07 | 5 | 4 | yes | yes | /guided-reading/series/moonwood-tales/book-07/page-004.webp | It boasts about Hollow Oak and secrets from the Deep Dark. |

| moonwood-tales-c-07 | 9 | 8 | yes | yes | /guided-reading/series/moonwood-tales/book-07/page-008.webp | The fish circles slowly. Being new made it want a grand past. |
| moonwood-tales-c-07 | 10 | 9 | yes | yes | /guided-reading/series/moonwood-tales/book-07/page-009.webp | Dewdrop points to its blue stripe. That true detail is already rare. |
| moonwood-tales-c-07 | 11 | 10 | yes | yes | /guided-reading/series/moonwood-tales/book-07/page-010.webp | The fish shows her a cold spring hidden below one root. |
| moonwood-tales-c-07 | 12 | 11 | yes | yes | /guided-reading/series/moonwood-tales/book-07/page-011.webp | After that, it shares small facts Dewdrop can test. |
| moonwood-tales-c-07 | 13 | 12 | yes | yes | /guided-reading/series/moonwood-tales/book-07/page-012.webp | "My name is Ripple," it tells Pip. One true story begins. |

| moonwood-tales-c-08 | 3 | 2 | yes | yes | /guided-reading/series/moonwood-tales/book-08/page-002.webp | He clears soil from a small underground door. |
| moonwood-tales-c-08 | 4 | 3 | yes | yes | /guided-reading/series/moonwood-tales/book-08/page-003.webp | Its carving shows one tree with roots reaching every direction. |
| moonwood-tales-c-08 | 5 | 4 | yes | yes | /guided-reading/series/moonwood-tales/book-08/page-004.webp | Burrow gathers friends, but pulling does not open it. |
| moonwood-tales-c-08 | 6 | 5 | yes | yes | /guided-reading/series/moonwood-tales/book-08/page-005.webp | Stone squeezes sideways while Luna folds her wings through the tunnel. |
| moonwood-tales-c-08 | 7 | 6 | yes | yes | /guided-reading/series/moonwood-tales/book-08/page-006.webp | Luna recognises the carving, though its opening rule escapes her. |
| moonwood-tales-c-08 | 8 | 7 | yes | yes | /guided-reading/series/moonwood-tales/book-08/page-007.webp | She traces each root. The door remains shut. |
| moonwood-tales-c-08 | 9 | 8 | yes | yes | /guided-reading/series/moonwood-tales/book-08/page-008.webp | Burrow matches the carved roots to the real roots overhead and presses north. |

| moonwood-tales-c-08 | 11 | 10 | yes | yes | /guided-reading/series/moonwood-tales/book-08/page-010.webp | Friends follow streams, creatures, and vanished paths around the walls. |
| moonwood-tales-c-08 | 12 | 11 | yes | yes | /guided-reading/series/moonwood-tales/book-08/page-011.webp | Luna remembers its name: the Memory Room. |
| moonwood-tales-c-08 | 13 | 12 | yes | yes | /guided-reading/series/moonwood-tales/book-08/page-012.webp | Burrow finds clear wall and draws today's Hollow Oak. |
| moonwood-tales-c-08 | 14 | 13 | yes | yes | /guided-reading/series/moonwood-tales/book-08/page-013.webp | The new picture glows beside the old ones. |
| moonwood-tales-c-09 | 2 | 1 | yes | yes | /guided-reading/series/moonwood-tales/book-09/page-001.webp | Spark catches a cold, and every sneeze carries loose wand magic. |
| moonwood-tales-c-09 | 3 | 2 | yes | yes | /guided-reading/series/moonwood-tales/book-09/page-002.webp | At breakfast, one sneeze turns porridge into a flying bird. |
| moonwood-tales-c-09 | 4 | 3 | yes | yes | /guided-reading/series/moonwood-tales/book-09/page-003.webp | Wren asks Spark to warn everyone before the next twitch. |
| moonwood-tales-c-09 | 5 | 4 | yes | yes | /guided-reading/series/moonwood-tales/book-09/page-004.webp | In Whispering Meadow, a sneeze turns every flower blue. |
| moonwood-tales-c-09 | 6 | 5 | yes | yes | /guided-reading/series/moonwood-tales/book-09/page-005.webp | Spark apologises and feels another tickle starting. |
| moonwood-tales-c-09 | 7 | 6 | yes | yes | /guided-reading/series/moonwood-tales/book-09/page-006.webp | He holds it in. Gold magic builds around his watering eyes. |
| moonwood-tales-c-09 | 8 | 7 | yes | yes | /guided-reading/series/moonwood-tales/book-09/page-007.webp | Fern points away from Crystal Stream and brings a cloth. |
| moonwood-tales-c-09 | 9 | 8 | yes | yes | /guided-reading/series/moonwood-tales/book-09/page-008.webp | The trapped sneeze escapes beside the water. |
| moonwood-tales-c-09 | 10 | 9 | yes | yes | /guided-reading/series/moonwood-tales/book-09/page-009.webp | Fish turn purple, the stream changes key, and one fish briefly becomes a frog. |
| moonwood-tales-c-09 | 11 | 10 | yes | yes | /guided-reading/series/moonwood-tales/book-09/page-010.webp | Spark stops practising magic and asks Dewdrop to count every changed fish. |
| moonwood-tales-c-09 | 12 | 11 | yes | yes | /guided-reading/series/moonwood-tales/book-09/page-011.webp | Dewdrop needs the fish restored before another spell is tested. |
| moonwood-tales-c-09 | 13 | 12 | yes | yes | /guided-reading/series/moonwood-tales/book-09/page-012.webp | Wren checks the remedy title, direction arrow, and exact dose. |
| moonwood-tales-c-09 | 14 | 13 | yes | yes | /guided-reading/series/moonwood-tales/book-09/page-013.webp | The remedy restores fish, flowers, water, and Spark's clear nose. |
| moonwood-tales-c-09 | 15 | 14 | yes | yes | /guided-reading/series/moonwood-tales/book-09/page-014.webp | Spark sneezes into the cloth. Nothing changes, so Dewdrop checks off every fish. |

| moonwood-tales-c-10 | 3 | 2 | yes | yes | /guided-reading/series/moonwood-tales/book-10/page-002.webp | She remembers only that it is outside and cannot wait. |
| moonwood-tales-c-10 | 4 | 3 | yes | yes | /guided-reading/series/moonwood-tales/book-10/page-003.webp | Pip asks for one sense. Luna remembers cold water. |
| moonwood-tales-c-10 | 5 | 4 | yes | yes | /guided-reading/series/moonwood-tales/book-10/page-004.webp | Fern and Burrow suggest places, but none feels right. |
| moonwood-tales-c-10 | 6 | 5 | yes | yes | /guided-reading/series/moonwood-tales/book-10/page-005.webp | They search Hollow Oak, every path, and Whispering Meadow without success. |
| moonwood-tales-c-10 | 7 | 6 | yes | yes | /guided-reading/series/moonwood-tales/book-10/page-006.webp | Dewdrop names Crystal Stream. Luna stops and faces the water. |
| moonwood-tales-c-10 | 8 | 7 | yes | yes | /guided-reading/series/moonwood-tales/book-10/page-007.webp | She flies to the stepping stones and studies the pebbles below. |
| moonwood-tales-c-10 | 9 | 8 | yes | yes | /guided-reading/series/moonwood-tales/book-10/page-008.webp | A silver box waits underwater where Luna once hid it. |
| moonwood-tales-c-10 | 10 | 9 | yes | yes | /guided-reading/series/moonwood-tales/book-10/page-009.webp | The cold box brings back the task: plant its contents today. |
| moonwood-tales-c-10 | 11 | 10 | yes | yes | /guided-reading/series/moonwood-tales/book-10/page-010.webp | Inside, twelve first-tree seeds glow with steady gold light. |
| moonwood-tales-c-10 | 12 | 11 | yes | yes | /guided-reading/series/moonwood-tales/book-10/page-011.webp | Luna explains the tired old trees need young neighbours. |
| moonwood-tales-c-10 | 13 | 12 | yes | yes | /guided-reading/series/moonwood-tales/book-10/page-012.webp | She gives one seed to each waiting Moonwood friend. |
| moonwood-tales-c-10 | 14 | 13 | yes | yes | /guided-reading/series/moonwood-tales/book-10/page-013.webp | They plant a ring around Hollow Oak before sunset. |
| moonwood-tales-c-10 | 15 | 14 | yes | yes | /guided-reading/series/moonwood-tales/book-10/page-014.webp | By night, twelve gold shoots mark the task Luna nearly missed. |
| moonwood-tales-c-11 | 2 | 1 | yes | yes | /guided-reading/series/moonwood-tales/book-11/page-001.webp | A huge sound rolls from Fog Marsh while Pip and Stone sit outside. |

| moonwood-tales-c-11 | 7 | 6 | yes | yes | /guided-reading/series/moonwood-tales/book-11/page-006.webp | Reeds shake and dark water ripples as the noise draws closer. |
| moonwood-tales-c-11 | 8 | 7 | yes | yes | /guided-reading/series/moonwood-tales/book-11/page-007.webp | One tiny toadling sits on a mossy rock with its mouth wide open. |

| moonwood-tales-c-11 | 11 | 10 | yes | yes | /guided-reading/series/moonwood-tales/book-11/page-010.webp | Stone kneels. "I am large, so I will call carefully." |

| moonwood-tales-c-11 | 13 | 12 | yes | yes | /guided-reading/series/moonwood-tales/book-11/page-012.webp | Back home, Pip and Stone hear the reunited marsh family singing. |

| moonwood-tales-c-12 | 3 | 2 | yes | yes | /guided-reading/series/moonwood-tales/book-12/page-002.webp | Dewdrop listens below the surface. The silence begins upstream. |
| moonwood-tales-c-12 | 4 | 3 | yes | yes | /guided-reading/series/moonwood-tales/book-12/page-003.webp | They follow the water toward its oldest source. |
| moonwood-tales-c-12 | 5 | 4 | yes | yes | /guided-reading/series/moonwood-tales/book-12/page-004.webp | Roots grow broader while the stream becomes quieter. |

| moonwood-tales-c-12 | 8 | 7 | yes | yes | /guided-reading/series/moonwood-tales/book-12/page-007.webp | Fern pushes with both hands. Mud grips the stone tighter. |

| moonwood-tales-c-12 | 10 | 9 | yes | yes | /guided-reading/series/moonwood-tales/book-12/page-009.webp | Vine pulls, water lifts, and the loosened boulder rolls aside. |
| moonwood-tales-c-12 | 11 | 10 | yes | yes | /guided-reading/series/moonwood-tales/book-12/page-010.webp | Current rushes over the stones until their clear notes return. |
| moonwood-tales-c-12 | 12 | 11 | yes | yes | /guided-reading/series/moonwood-tales/book-12/page-011.webp | Fern and Dewdrop sit beside the song they restored together. |
| moonwood-tales-c-12 | 13 | 12 | yes | yes | /guided-reading/series/moonwood-tales/book-12/page-012.webp | At Hollow Oak, Pip hears the stream before he sees them. |
